2026-10-12T07:20:51.9122364Z  	CREATE FUNCTION "round_migration_16fa94701bfe4355a8a66888688dacd5".capture_workflow_round() RETURNS trigger
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9122557Z  	LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path=pg_catalog AS $$
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9122961Z  	DECLARE previous "round_migration_16fa94701bfe4355a8a66888688dacd5".workflow_rounds%ROWTYPE; next_number bigint; parent_state text;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9123041Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9123223Z  	 IF current_setting('transaction_isolation')<>'read committed' THEN
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9123508Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='40001',MESSAGE='workflow round allocation requires read committed';
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9123584Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9124020Z  	 PERFORM 1 FROM "round_migration_16fa94701bfe4355a8a66888688dacd5".workflow_definitions WHERE app_id=NEW.app_id AND id=NEW.flow_id FOR UPDATE;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9124328Z  	 IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='23503',MESSAGE='workflow round definition missing'; END IF;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9124587Z  	 SELECT * INTO previous FROM "round_migration_16fa94701bfe4355a8a66888688dacd5".workflow_rounds
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9124787Z  	  WHERE app_id=NEW.app_id AND flow_id=NEW.flow_id AND record_id=NEW.record_id
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9124893Z  	  ORDER BY round_number DESC LIMIT 1;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9125032Z  	 next_number:=COALESCE(previous.round_number,0)+1;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9125393Z  	 IF next_number>9007199254740991 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='40001',MESSAGE='workflow round limit reached'; END IF;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9125513Z  	 IF NEW.round_kind IN ('resubmit','review') THEN
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9125876Z  	  IF previous.instance_id IS NULL OR previous.instance_id<>NEW.previous_instance_id OR previous.table_id<>NEW.table_id THEN
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9126128Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='40001',MESSAGE='workflow parent is not the latest round';
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9126208Z  	  END IF;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9126661Z  	  SELECT state INTO parent_state FROM "round_migration_16fa94701bfe4355a8a66888688dacd5".workflow_instances WHERE id=previous.instance_id FOR SHARE;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9126895Z  	  IF parent_state IS NULL OR parent_state NOT IN ('completed','rejected','withdrawn') OR
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9127392Z  	     (NEW.round_kind='resubmit' AND (parent_state NOT IN ('rejected','withdrawn') OR previous.initiator_id<>NEW.initiator_id)) THEN
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9127617Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='40001',MESSAGE='workflow parent is not eligible';
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9127696Z  	  END IF;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9127769Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9128469Z  	 INSERT INTO "round_migration_16fa94701bfe4355a8a66888688dacd5".workflow_rounds(instance_id,app_id,table_id,view_id,record_id,flow_id,initiator_id,definition_version,round_number,previous_instance_id,round_kind,created_at)
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9129041Z  	 VALUES(NEW.id,NEW.app_id,NEW.table_id,NEW.view_id,NEW.record_id,NEW.flow_id,NEW.initiator_id,NEW.definition_version,next_number,NEW.previous_instance_id,NEW.round_kind,NEW.created_at);
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9129123Z  	 RETURN NEW;
2026-10-12T07:20:51.9129197Z  	END $$;
